^SAMPLE = GSM5027631
!Sample_title = O1_failed_to_transiently_reprogram_13days_exp1
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM5027631
!Sample_status = Public on Apr 19 2022
!Sample_submission_date = Jan 20 2021
!Sample_last_update_date = Apr 19 2022
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Failed to transiently reprogram fibroblasts
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Failed to transiently reprogram fibroblasts
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = length of reprogramming phase (days): 13
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = donor age (years): 53
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblasts from middle age donors (38-53 years old) were purchased from Lonza. For lentiviral iPSC reprogramming, fibroblasts were expanded in fibroblast medium (DMEM-F12, 10% FBS, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 16 ng/ml FGF2) before being spinfected with tetO-GFP-hOKMS and M2rtTA lentiviruses, where 10% virus supernatant and 8 µg/ml polybrene was added to the cells before centrifugation at 1000 rpm for 60 minutes at 32°C. Reprogramming was initiated 24 hours after lentiviral transduction by introducing doxycycline (2 µg/ml) to the media. Media was changed daily throughout the experiment subsequently. On day 2 of reprogramming, cells were flow sorted for viable GFP positive cells and then cultured on gelatine coated plates. On day 7 of reprogramming, cells were replated onto irradiated mouse embryonic fibroblasts (iMEFs) and on day 8 of reprogramming, the medium was switched to hES medium (DMEM-F12, 20% KSR, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 8 ng/ml FGF2). For transient reprogramming, cells were flow sorted at day 10, 13, 15 or 17 of reprogramming for the CD13+ SSEA4- and CD13- SSEA4+ populations. These cells were then replated on iMEFs in fibroblast medium without doxycycline and subsequently maintained like fibroblasts without iMEFs.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was extracted from cell samples with the RNeasy mini kit (Qiagen) by following the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA-Seq libraries were prepared at the Wellcome Sanger Institute and run on a HiSeq 2500 system (Illumina) for 50 bp single-end sequencing.
!Sample_data_processing = Reads were trimmed with Trim Galore (version 0.6.2) and aligned to the human genome (GRCh38) with Hisat2 (version 2.1.0).
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: SeqMonk Annotated Probe reports of all genes with log2 RPM values in each sample

!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Failed to transiently reprogram fibroblasts
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Failed to transiently reprogram fibroblasts
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = length of reprogramming phase (days): 15
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = donor age (years): 53
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblasts from middle age donors (38-53 years old) were purchased from Lonza. For lentiviral iPSC reprogramming, fibroblasts were expanded in fibroblast medium (DMEM-F12, 10% FBS, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 16 ng/ml FGF2) before being spinfected with tetO-GFP-hOKMS and M2rtTA lentiviruses, where 10% virus supernatant and 8 µg/ml polybrene was added to the cells before centrifugation at 1000 rpm for 60 minutes at 32°C. Reprogramming was initiated 24 hours after lentiviral transduction by introducing doxycycline (2 µg/ml) to the media. Media was changed daily throughout the experiment subsequently. On day 2 of reprogramming, cells were flow sorted for viable GFP positive cells and then cultured on gelatine coated plates. On day 7 of reprogramming, cells were replated onto irradiated mouse embryonic fibroblasts (iMEFs) and on day 8 of reprogramming, the medium was switched to hES medium (DMEM-F12, 20% KSR, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 8 ng/ml FGF2). For transient reprogramming, cells were flow sorted at day 10, 13, 15 or 17 of reprogramming for the CD13+ SSEA4- and CD13- SSEA4+ populations. These cells were then replated on iMEFs in fibroblast medium without doxycycline and subsequently maintained like fibroblasts without iMEFs.

!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Failed to transiently reprogram fibroblasts
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Failed to transiently reprogram fibroblasts
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = length of reprogramming phase (days): 13
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = donor age (years): 38
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblasts from middle age donors (38-53 years old) were purchased from Lonza. For lentiviral iPSC reprogramming, fibroblasts were expanded in fibroblast medium (DMEM-F12, 10% FBS, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 16 ng/ml FGF2) before being spinfected with tetO-GFP-hOKMS and M2rtTA lentiviruses, where 10% virus supernatant and 8 µg/ml polybrene was added to the cells before centrifugation at 1000 rpm for 60 minutes at 32°C. Reprogramming was initiated 24 hours after lentiviral transduction by introducing doxycycline (2 µg/ml) to the media. Media was changed daily throughout the experiment subsequently. On day 2 of reprogramming, cells were flow sorted for viable GFP positive cells and then cultured on gelatine coated plates. On day 7 of reprogramming, cells were replated onto irradiated mouse embryonic fibroblasts (iMEFs) and on day 8 of reprogramming, the medium was switched to hES medium (DMEM-F12, 20% KSR, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 8 ng/ml FGF2). For transient reprogramming, cells were flow sorted at day 10, 13, 15 or 17 of reprogramming for the CD13+ SSEA4- and CD13- SSEA4+ populations. These cells were then replated on iMEFs in fibroblast medium without doxycycline and subsequently maintained like fibroblasts without iMEFs.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was extracted from cell samples with the RNeasy mini kit (Qiagen) by following the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA-Seq libraries were prepared at the Wellcome Sanger Institute and run on a HiSeq 2500 system (Illumina) for 50 bp single-end sequencing.
!Sample_data_processing = Reads were trimmed with Trim Galore (version 0.6.2) and aligned to the human genome (GRCh38) with Hisat2 (version 2.1.0).
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary_files_format_and_content: SeqMonk Annotated Probe reports of all genes with log2 RPM values in each sample

!Sample_title = O3_transiently_reprogrammed_17days_exp1
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM5027654
!Sample_status = Public on Apr 19 2022
!Sample_submission_date = Jan 20 2021
!Sample_last_update_date = Apr 19 2022
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Transiently reprogrammed fibroblasts
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Transiently reprogrammed fibroblasts
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = length of reprogramming phase (days): 17
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = donor age (years): 38
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblasts from middle age donors (38-53 years old) were purchased from Lonza. For lentiviral iPSC reprogramming, fibroblasts were expanded in fibroblast medium (DMEM-F12, 10% FBS, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 16 ng/ml FGF2) before being spinfected with tetO-GFP-hOKMS and M2rtTA lentiviruses, where 10% virus supernatant and 8 µg/ml polybrene was added to the cells before centrifugation at 1000 rpm for 60 minutes at 32°C. Reprogramming was initiated 24 hours after lentiviral transduction by introducing doxycycline (2 µg/ml) to the media. Media was changed daily throughout the experiment subsequently. On day 2 of reprogramming, cells were flow sorted for viable GFP positive cells and then cultured on gelatine coated plates. On day 7 of reprogramming, cells were replated onto irradiated mouse embryonic fibroblasts (iMEFs) and on day 8 of reprogramming, the medium was switched to hES medium (DMEM-F12, 20% KSR, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 8 ng/ml FGF2). For transient reprogramming, cells were flow sorted at day 10, 13, 15 or 17 of reprogramming for the CD13+ SSEA4- and CD13- SSEA4+ populations. These cells were then replated on iMEFs in fibroblast medium without doxycycline and subsequently maintained like fibroblasts without iMEFs.

On day 2 of reprogramming, cells were flow sorted for viable GFP positive cells and then cultured on gelatine coated plates. On day 7 of reprogramming, cells were replated onto irradiated mouse embryonic fibroblasts (iMEFs) and on day 8 of reprogramming, the medium was switched to hES medium (DMEM-F12, 20% KSR, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 8 ng/ml FGF2). For transient reprogramming, cells were flow sorted at day 10, 13, 15 or 17 of reprogramming for the CD13+ SSEA4- and CD13- SSEA4+ populations. These cells were then replated on iMEFs in fibroblast medium without doxycycline and subsequently maintained like fibroblasts without iMEFs.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was extracted from cell samples with the RNeasy mini kit (Qiagen) by following the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA-Seq libraries were prepared at the Wellcome Sanger Institute and run on a HiSeq 2500 system (Illumina) for 50 bp single-end sequencing.
!Sample_data_processing = Reads were trimmed with Trim Galore (version 0.6.2) and aligned to the human genome (GRCh38) with HISAT2 (version 2.1.0).
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = RNA-seq report: SeqMonk report with log2 RPM values for each each gene.

For lentiviral iPSC reprogramming, fibroblasts were expanded in fibroblast medium (DMEM-F12, 10% FBS, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 16 ng/ml FGF2) before being spinfected with tetO-GFP-hOKMS and M2rtTA lentiviruses, where 10% virus supernatant and 8 µg/ml polybrene was added to the cells before centrifugation at 1000 rpm for 60 minutes at 32°C. Reprogramming was initiated 24 hours after lentiviral transduction by introducing doxycycline (2 µg/ml) to the media. Media was changed daily throughout the experiment subsequently. On day 2 of reprogramming, cells were flow sorted for viable GFP positive cells and then cultured on gelatine coated plates. On day 7 of reprogramming, cells were replated onto irradiated mouse embryonic fibroblasts (iMEFs) and on day 8 of reprogramming, the medium was switched to hES medium (DMEM-F12, 20% KSR, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 8 ng/ml FGF2). For transient reprogramming, cells were flow sorted at day 10, 13, 15 or 17 of reprogramming for the CD13+ SSEA4- and CD13- SSEA4+ populations. These cells were then replated on iMEFs in fibroblast medium without doxycycline and subsequently maintained like fibroblasts without iMEFs.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was extracted from cell samples with the RNeasy mini kit (Qiagen) by following the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA-Seq libraries were prepared at the Wellcome Sanger Institute and run on a HiSeq 2500 system (Illumina) for 50 bp single-end sequencing.
!Sample_data_processing = Reads were trimmed with Trim Galore (version 0.6.2) and aligned to the human genome (GRCh38) with HISAT2 (version 2.1.0).
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = RNA-seq report: SeqMonk report with log2 RPM values for each each gene.

!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Fibroblast
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Transient reprogramming intermediate
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = length of reprogramming phase (days): 15
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = donor age (years): 53
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblasts from middle age donors (38-53 years old) were purchased from Lonza. For lentiviral iPSC reprogramming, fibroblasts were expanded in fibroblast medium (DMEM-F12, 10% FBS, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 16 ng/ml FGF2) before being spinfected with tetO-GFP-hOKMS and M2rtTA lentiviruses, where 10% virus supernatant and 8 µg/ml polybrene was added to the cells before centrifugation at 1000 rpm for 60 minutes at 32°C. Reprogramming was initiated 24 hours after lentiviral transduction by introducing doxycycline (2 µg/ml) to the media. Media was changed daily throughout the experiment subsequently. On day 2 of reprogramming, cells were flow sorted for viable GFP positive cells and then cultured on gelatine coated plates. On day 7 of reprogramming, cells were replated onto irradiated mouse embryonic fibroblasts (iMEFs) and on day 8 of reprogramming, the medium was switched to hES medium (DMEM-F12, 20% KSR, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 8 ng/ml FGF2). For transient reprogramming, cells were flow sorted at day 10, 13, 15 or 17 of reprogramming for the CD13+ SSEA4- and CD13- SSEA4+ populations. These cells were then replated on iMEFs in fibroblast medium without doxycycline and subsequently maintained like fibroblasts without iMEFs.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was extracted from cell samples with the RNeasy mini kit (Qiagen) by following the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA-Seq libraries were prepared at the Wellcome Sanger Institute and run on a HiSeq 2500 system (Illumina) for 50 bp single-end sequencing.
!Sample_data_processing = Reads were trimmed with Trim Galore (version 0.6.2) and aligned to the human genome (GRCh38) with HISAT2 (version 2.1.0).
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = RNA-seq report: SeqMonk report with log2 RPM values for each each gene.
!Sample_platform_id = GPL20301
!Sample_contact_name = Felix,,Krueger

^SAMPLE = GSM5391813
!Sample_title = O2_failing_to_transiently_reprogram_intermediate_15days_exp2
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM5391813
!Sample_status = Public on Apr 19 2022
!Sample_submission_date = Jun 18 2021
!Sample_last_update_date = Apr 19 2022
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Fibroblast
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Failing to transiently reprogram intermediate
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = length of reprogramming phase (days): 15
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = donor age (years): 53
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblasts from middle age donors (38-53 years old) were purchased from Lonza. For lentiviral iPSC reprogramming, fibroblasts were expanded in fibroblast medium (DMEM-F12, 10% FBS, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 16 ng/ml FGF2) before being spinfected with tetO-GFP-hOKMS and M2rtTA lentiviruses, where 10% virus supernatant and 8 µg/ml polybrene was added to the cells before centrifugation at 1000 rpm for 60 minutes at 32°C. Reprogramming was initiated 24 hours after lentiviral transduction by introducing doxycycline (2 µg/ml) to the media. Media was changed daily throughout the experiment subsequently. On day 2 of reprogramming, cells were flow sorted for viable GFP positive cells and then cultured on gelatine coated plates. On day 7 of reprogramming, cells were replated onto irradiated mouse embryonic fibroblasts (iMEFs) and on day 8 of reprogramming, the medium was switched to hES medium (DMEM-F12, 20% KSR, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 8 ng/ml FGF2). For transient reprogramming, cells were flow sorted at day 10, 13, 15 or 17 of reprogramming for the CD13+ SSEA4- and CD13- SSEA4+ populations. These cells were then replated on iMEFs in fibroblast medium without doxycycline and subsequently maintained like fibroblasts without iMEFs.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was extracted from cell samples with the RNeasy mini kit (Qiagen) by following the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA-Seq libraries were prepared at the Wellcome Sanger Institute and run on a HiSeq 2500 system (Illumina) for 50 bp single-end sequencing.
!Sample_data_processing = Reads were trimmed with Trim Galore (version 0.6.2) and aligned to the human genome (GRCh38) with HISAT2 (version 2.1.0).
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = RNA-seq report: SeqMonk report with log2 RPM values for each each gene.
!Sample_platform_id = GPL20301

!Sample_title = O2_negative_control_intermediate_13days_exp2
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM5391821
!Sample_status = Public on Apr 19 2022
!Sample_submission_date = Jun 18 2021
!Sample_last_update_date = Apr 19 2022
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Fibroblast
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Negative control intermediate
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = length of reprogramming phase (days): 13
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = donor age (years): 53
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblasts from middle age donors (38-53 years old) were purchased from Lonza. For lentiviral iPSC reprogramming, fibroblasts were expanded in fibroblast medium (DMEM-F12, 10% FBS, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 16 ng/ml FGF2) before being spinfected with tetO-GFP-hOKMS and M2rtTA lentiviruses, where 10% virus supernatant and 8 µg/ml polybrene was added to the cells before centrifugation at 1000 rpm for 60 minutes at 32°C. Reprogramming was initiated 24 hours after lentiviral transduction by introducing doxycycline (2 µg/ml) to the media. Media was changed daily throughout the experiment subsequently. On day 2 of reprogramming, cells were flow sorted for viable GFP positive cells and then cultured on gelatine coated plates. On day 7 of reprogramming, cells were replated onto irradiated mouse embryonic fibroblasts (iMEFs) and on day 8 of reprogramming, the medium was switched to hES medium (DMEM-F12, 20% KSR, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 8 ng/ml FGF2). For transient reprogramming, cells were flow sorted at day 10, 13, 15 or 17 of reprogramming for the CD13+ SSEA4- and CD13- SSEA4+ populations. These cells were then replated on iMEFs in fibroblast medium without doxycycline and subsequently maintained like fibroblasts without iMEFs.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was extracted from cell samples with the RNeasy mini kit (Qiagen) by following the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA-Seq libraries were prepared at the Wellcome Sanger Institute and run on a HiSeq 2500 system (Illumina) for 50 bp single-end sequencing.
!Sample_data_processing = Reads were trimmed with Trim Galore (version 0.6.2) and aligned to the human genome (GRCh38) with HISAT2 (version 2.1.0).
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = RNA-seq report: SeqMonk report with log2 RPM values for each each gene.

!Sample_title = O2_transient_reprogramming_intermediate_17days_exp2
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM5391829
!Sample_status = Public on Apr 19 2022
!Sample_submission_date = Jun 18 2021
!Sample_last_update_date = Apr 19 2022
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Fibroblast
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Transient reprogramming intermediate
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = length of reprogramming phase (days): 17
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = donor age (years): 53
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblasts from middle age donors (38-53 years old) were purchased from Lonza. For lentiviral iPSC reprogramming, fibroblasts were expanded in fibroblast medium (DMEM-F12, 10% FBS, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 16 ng/ml FGF2) before being spinfected with tetO-GFP-hOKMS and M2rtTA lentiviruses, where 10% virus supernatant and 8 µg/ml polybrene was added to the cells before centrifugation at 1000 rpm for 60 minutes at 32°C. Reprogramming was initiated 24 hours after lentiviral transduction by introducing doxycycline (2 µg/ml) to the media. Media was changed daily throughout the experiment subsequently. On day 2 of reprogramming, cells were flow sorted for viable GFP positive cells and then cultured on gelatine coated plates. On day 7 of reprogramming, cells were replated onto irradiated mouse embryonic fibroblasts (iMEFs) and on day 8 of reprogramming, the medium was switched to hES medium (DMEM-F12, 20% KSR, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 8 ng/ml FGF2). For transient reprogramming, cells were flow sorted at day 10, 13, 15 or 17 of reprogramming for the CD13+ SSEA4- and CD13- SSEA4+ populations. These cells were then replated on iMEFs in fibroblast medium without doxycycline and subsequently maintained like fibroblasts without iMEFs.

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was extracted from cell samples with the RNeasy mini kit (Qiagen) by following the manufacturer’s instructions.

For transient reprogramming, cells were flow sorted at day 10, 13, 15 or 17 of reprogramming for the CD13+ SSEA4- and CD13- SSEA4+ populations. These cells were then replated on iMEFs in fibroblast medium without doxycycline and subsequently maintained like fibroblasts without iMEFs.

!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblasts from middle age donors (38-53 years old) were purchased from Lonza. For lentiviral iPSC reprogramming, fibroblasts were expanded in fibroblast medium (DMEM-F12, 10% FBS, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 16 ng/ml FGF2) before being spinfected with tetO-GFP-hOKMS and M2rtTA lentiviruses, where 10% virus supernatant and 8 µg/ml polybrene was added to the cells before centrifugation at 1000 rpm for 60 minutes at 32°C. Reprogramming was initiated 24 hours after lentiviral transduction by introducing doxycycline (2 µg/ml) to the media. Media was changed daily throughout the experiment subsequently. On day 2 of reprogramming, cells were flow sorted for viable GFP positive cells and then cultured on gelatine coated plates. On day 7 of reprogramming, cells were replated onto irradiated mouse embryonic fibroblasts (iMEFs) and on day 8 of reprogramming, the medium was switched to hES medium (DMEM-F12, 20% KSR, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 8 ng/ml FGF2). For transient reprogramming, cells were flow sorted at day 10, 13, 15 or 17 of reprogramming for the CD13+ SSEA4- and CD13- SSEA4+ populations. These cells were then replated on iMEFs in fibroblast medium without doxycycline and subsequently maintained like fibroblasts without iMEFs.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was extracted from cell samples with the RNeasy mini kit (Qiagen) by following the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA-Seq libraries were prepared at the Wellcome Sanger Institute and run on a HiSeq 2500 system (Illumina) for 50 bp single-end sequencing.
!Sample_data_processing = Reads were trimmed with Trim Galore (version 0.6.2) and aligned to the human genome (GRCh38) with HISAT2 (version 2.1.0).
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = RNA-seq report: SeqMonk report with log2 RPM values for each each gene.

!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Fibroblast
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Transient reprogramming intermediate
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = length of reprogramming phase (days): 13
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = donor age (years): 38
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblasts from middle age donors (38-53 years old) were purchased from Lonza. For lentiviral iPSC reprogramming, fibroblasts were expanded in fibroblast medium (DMEM-F12, 10% FBS, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 16 ng/ml FGF2) before being spinfected with tetO-GFP-hOKMS and M2rtTA lentiviruses, where 10% virus supernatant and 8 µg/ml polybrene was added to the cells before centrifugation at 1000 rpm for 60 minutes at 32°C. Reprogramming was initiated 24 hours after lentiviral transduction by introducing doxycycline (2 µg/ml) to the media. Media was changed daily throughout the experiment subsequently. On day 2 of reprogramming, cells were flow sorted for viable GFP positive cells and then cultured on gelatine coated plates. On day 7 of reprogramming, cells were replated onto irradiated mouse embryonic fibroblasts (iMEFs) and on day 8 of reprogramming, the medium was switched to hES medium (DMEM-F12, 20% KSR, 1X Glutamax, 1X MEM-NEAA, 1X beta-mercaptoethanol, 0.2X Penicillin/Streptomycin, 8 ng/ml FGF2). For transient reprogramming, cells were flow sorted at day 10, 13, 15 or 17 of reprogramming for the CD13+ SSEA4- and CD13- SSEA4+ populations. These cells were then replated on iMEFs in fibroblast medium without doxycycline and subsequently maintained like fibroblasts without iMEFs.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was extracted from cell samples with the RNeasy mini kit (Qiagen) by following the manufacturer’s instructions.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA-Seq libraries were prepared at the Wellcome Sanger Institute and run on a HiSeq 2500 system (Illumina) for 50 bp single-end sequencing.
!Sample_data_processing = Reads were trimmed with Trim Galore (version 0.6.2) and aligned to the human genome (GRCh38) with HISAT2 (version 2.1.0).
!Sample_data_processing = Genome_build: GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = RNA-seq report: SeqMonk report with log2 RPM values for each each gene.
